90-100%，它一次调用要写全部三份文档 spec/plan/tasks ~62KB），第 4 轮由**我们自己的包装器**（timeoutMs+2s 宽限，早于 pi-subagents 内部子期限触发，因 child 启动偏移 >2s）取消，而 cancel-settle 在 pi-subagents 侧是**异步**的（controller.abort → in-flight child reject → finally 才移除 active node，观测窗口 <1s）——第 5 轮在 **182ms 后**重派发，nodeId 是 `pipeline.${agent}` **逐字确定性**（delegation-backend.ts:280，头注释自称 "per-call id" 但从未有调用唯一成分），(ownerRunId, nodeId) 仍在 activeOwnedNodes → 终端 `duplicate_node`（43ms 拒绝，model=unknown，零 usage）→ v0.3.65 熔断计为第 3 个连续 agent error → FatalAbort。**类修三件**：(1) **每次尝试唯一 nodeId**（`<base>@<requestId>`，requestId 本就每次生成）——逻辑基名保留在前缀（日志/调用方 id 语义不变），任何取消/超时后的重试在结构上不可能与仍在 settle 的前驱碰撞；cancel 事件携带同一组合值（身份元组精确性钉测）；(2) **duplicate_node 有界重试**（双保险）：终端 duplicate_node（或桥接拒绝文本点名）→ 一次退避重试（`SUPER_DEV_DUPLICATE_NODE_RETRY_MS` 默认 2000；该次尝试从未启动，重试零成本——43ms 拒绝、零 usage），第二次仍 duplicate 保持诚实错误（持续性故障归 v0.3.65 熔断）；(3) **spec-writer 30 分钟 heavy-writer 档**（`SUPER_DEV_WRITER_TIMEOUT_MS`，默认 1800000 = 最差观测完成 +50% 余量，与 v0.3.73 M4 reviewer 档同校准法；证据外科：其余文档角色每次观测都在 20 分钟内舒适完成，保持默认档）。**同波密封修复（M5 类，事故当场被用户 config 触发）**：用户在 `~/.super-dev/config.json` env 设 `SUPER_DEV_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS=3600000` 解锁 resume 的同时，tests/agent-runtime.test.ts 的默认档断言（research-agent 1_200_000）开始收到 **3_600_000**——泄漏链：该文件的本地 `vi.mock(super-dev-dir)` spread `...actual` **恢复了真 superDevEnv**（覆盖 setup 的全suite stub），而真 superDevEnv→envConfigCached→getConfig 是**模块内本地绑定**（namespace mock 拦不住，v0.3.73 CR 同型），config.env 畅通读到家目录真配置——v0.3.15 以来休眠，今天配置一改即破。类修：superDevEnv 实现内部加 `SUPER_DEV_NO_CONFIG_ENV=1` kill-switch（生产永不设置，优先级不变；vitest setup 全局钉死）——~10 个本地 mock 文件全部一次性密封，未来新增同样免疫（twin 同步 + 语义测试：env 仍胜 config、开关抑制 config 回退、本地解除恢复生产语义）。测试：tests/delegation-duplicate-node.test.ts 7 项（nodeId 唯一性/基名前缀/纠正重试新 id/cancel 身份元组/duplicate 一次重试成功/双 duplicate 有界诚实错/完成零退避）、tests/run-audit-fixes.test.ts +3（writer 档默认/外科性/env 覆盖且 DEFAULT 不泄漏）、tests/agent-runtime.test.ts 档位钉更新、tests/delegation-backend.test.ts nodeId 契约更新（同基名新后缀）、tests/config-env.test.ts kill-switch twin 测试 + 局部解除；全套件 243 文件 3495 passed + 1 skipped，tsc clean，coverage 92.58%/81.27%/95.05%（85/80 门之上）。运维注记：config env 的 3600000 解锁有效（resume run 实测 `timeout=3600000ms` 生效且健康），但它抬高全部 ~15 个默认档角色的失败检测延迟（卡死 agent 3×60min 才熔断 vs 校准后 3×20min）——本修落地后建议移除该 override 让所有角色回到校准档（spec-writer 30min 档独立生效）。 **双评审修入（dual review 2026-09-09，C1/C2/N1/ADV-F4/F5）**：duplicate_node 退避钳制 60s 且 signal-aware（父级已 abort 则跳过重试、诚实返回 duplicate 错误，实际等待值落入进度行 P10）；生产 kill-switch 行由 config-env.test.ts source-contract 钉死（twin 测试拦不住删守卫）；外科档测试改钉真实角色（bdd-scenario-writer/handoff-writer）；README env 表补 SUPER_DEV_NO_CONFIG_ENV 行；v0.3.84 describe 从 v0.3.74 中解嵌。两位评审对核心修均 verdict 通过（0.64/0.65.1/0.66.0 tarball 对照验证 nodeId @ 形状合法、(ownerRunId,nodeId) 键控、≤4 次尝试有界、无 usage 洗账）。
- **v0.3.83（resume 缓存永不重放不可恢复的失败 — 毒条目类级修复）** — 事故 2026-09-08（pi-omosis spec 25，run 13-21-29-124Z + 13-26-29-875Z）：zai 5h 配额窗口 16:15 封顶时 requirements/assessment/design/designReview 四个调用的**失败 AgentResult（`{error, text:"", control:null}`）被无条件 append 进 `.resume-cache.jsonl`**；此后每一次 resume 都被 memoizer 命中缓存键**原样重放 16:15 的错误**——配额 20:12 已重置、exclusion store 已删除、pi 已重启，对 append-only 的缓存 Map 全部不可见（重放零次模型调用，永不自愈；错误文本里的 `expires:` 时间戳从不被解析或比较）。基础设施恢复三连（删 store/重启/重跑）全部无效后，靠手工从缓存文件剥出 4 条毒行才解锁——毒的载体是 super-dev 自己的缓存，不是进程内存也不是磁盘 store。**读端修复（自愈存量）**：缓存命中若是 error 且无 control，先走 v0.3.48 文本恢复（保留——解析边界改进后错误行可复活为成功）；恢复不出 control 则**视为 miss、live 重跑**（诚实日志 `cached agent error NOT replayed; re-running live (infrastructure may have healed)`），新结果行在 append-only last-wins 日志里天然遮蔽旧错误行——旧缓存文件无需手术。**写端修复（不产新毒）**：`realAgent` 返回纯失败（error + 无 control + 无正文文本）时**不写缓存、不落盘**——失败不是"结果"而是"没出结果"；error 但有正文仍可缓存（v0.3.48 恢复路径的原料）。**时间类故障天然自愈**：配额窗口/瞬态基础设施失败后，下一次 resume 恰好重试一次 live（有界：每次 resume 至多一次重试，成功后新行接管）；缓存中已有的存量毒行在第一次 resume 即自动降级为 miss。语义不变项：成功结果重放原样（resume 的价值本体）、v0.3.48 恢复优先于 live 重跑、`round > priorRounds` replay 守卫保留（变冗余但无害）、live 失败的 3-连续 FatalAbort 不变（真失败该停就停）。测试：tests/resume.test.ts +6（读端三态：文本恢复仍优先/纯错误降级为 miss 且新成功行接管/成功行仍原样重放；写端两态：纯失败不持久化、带正文 error 仍持久化），全套件 242 文件 3475 passed + 1 skipped，tsc clean。根因分类：P10（缓存把"失败"当"结果"记账）× A+D（无时间维度的结构寻址使恢复不可见）。**双新鲜度评审修复（sd-code-reviewer Approved-with-Comments 2×LOW+1×NIT；sd-adversarial-reviewer CONTEST 2×MED+2×LOW+3×NIT，真实探针验证 /tmp/review-results/v0383-adv.md）**：(adv-F1 MED，探针 k5 实证) 委托纠正重试的基建失败产出 `{text, control, error}` 行（delegation-backend.ts:505/:508 携带首次尝试的**已被引擎判废的 control**）——读端降级门此前要求 `control == null`，该毒株**逐字重放永不过期**且同时逃过两个逃生阀（v0.3.65 agent-error 标记需 `!control`；v0.3.83 miss 路径需 `control==null`）。修复：读端门改为**任意 error 行**（文本恢复优先，陈旧 control 一律丢弃）；写端对 error 行的**持久化副本剥离 control**（live 调用方保留原始结果；error+control+无文本 → 剥离后即纯失败，不落盘）。(adv-F2 MED，机制实证) `countStageRounds` 仍把毒 error 行计入 priorRounds → 降级为 live 的重跑落在 `round ≤ priorRounds` 窗口内，3-连续 FatalAbort 熔断对它们失明——毒化 k-轮缓存每次 resume 最多重花 k 次**未计数的** live 调用且熔断不触发（探针：5 轮毒行 → fatal 延迟到第 8 轮）。修复：error 行是洞不是已入账工作——countStageRounds 只数成功行，毒洞以 FRESH 轮重执行（熔断第 3 轮即触发、预算照常计数；预算数学不变：fresh 配额 = effectiveCap − prior 与旧值一致）。(adv-F3 LOW) 写端注释谎称 error+文本行"免重花"——如实改为"保留恢复机会；不可恢复期间每次 resume 恰一次 live 重跑，成功行遮蔽为止（有界）"。(adv-F4 LOW) 全纯失败 pass 不再产生缓存文件 → isResumable=false、--resume/findReusableSpec 跳过该轨道（配额痊愈后无可恢复物）——修复：首个跳过持久化时写入惰性墓碑行（`__tombstone__@boot#1`，可解析、无 error、无调用键冲突、文件非空）保持轨道可发现。(code-F1 LOW) 空白-only 文本行为变更两侧均未钉——补测（不持久化 + 读端降级 live）。NIT×3：错位注释缩进、测试冗余 text 覆写、error+control 重放无毒标记（随读端修复自动消失——现在走 NOT-replayed 日志行）。测试：tests/resume.test.ts +8（读端毒株降级/文本恢复优先、写端剥离/剥离后纯失败、空白文本、countStageRounds 排除两态、墓碑可恢复）、tests/artifact-convergence.test.ts +1（error 种子轮 → 熔断第 3 轮而非第 8 轮）；全套件 242 文件 3484 passed + 1 skipped，tsc clean，coverage 92.63%/81.26%（85/80 门之上）。
- **v0.3.82（机械工具合并 + all-tools 布尔模式 — 删除 commonExtensionTools / agentExtensionTools）** — v0.3.78 双审修复引入的两张人工同步表被彻底删除：此前“声明扩展”（commonExtensions/agentExtensions）与“声明工具名”（commonExtensionTools/agentExtensionTools）必须人工保持一致，漏列即静默丢失全部扩展工具（正是 adv-F1 的原始缺陷形态）。新机制（三层）：(1) **激活期 toolIndex** — extension.ts 激活时从 `pi.getAllTools()` 一次性读取全部工具，经 `sourceInfo` 按包归属（`npm:pi-lsp` → lsp_* 家族，`npm:pi-mcp-adapter` → mcp/mcp__*）构建 `Map<package, toolNames[]>`；pi 按字母序先于本扩展加载 pi-lsp/pi-mcp-adapter 等急切注册扩展，激活时刻全量可见；(2) **注册期机械合并** — configExtensionToolsForAgent 重写为纯机械：extensionPackagesForAgent（role-hardcoded ∪ commonExtensions ∪ agentExtensions[role]，mechanical 分类器同 scope 排除）声明的每个包，其 toolIndex 中全部工具并入该角色 allowlist（Set 去重）——**声明即生效，零维护、零漂移**；(3) **allTools 布尔模式**（替代 "*" 字符串通配）— `allTools: true`（能力角色；mechanical 分类器排除）或 `agentAllTools: {role: true}`（任意角色，显式胜 scope，`sd-` 前缀键接受）→ 注册省略 tools pin（pi 的 allowedToolNames 是精确匹配 Set，省略才是唯一正确的“全部工具”），child 可见全部工具含未来新增的 `mcp__*` 直连工具；只读角色经 excludeTools 保留 write 家族隔离（绑定层只读强制仍是引擎侧 source boundary）。旧键残留于既有 config.json 中被无害忽略（钉测）。**已接受的取舍**：工具粒度从 per-tool 降为 per-package（声明 pi-lsp 即给全部 5 个 lsp_* 工具，无法只给 lsp_diagnostics）——live config 历来只列全集，无实际损失；换来消灭两表漂移这一缺陷源。
**双新鲜度评审修复（sd-code-reviewer Changes Requested 2×HIGH+2×MED；sd-adversarial-reviewer REJECT 1×BLOCKER+2×HIGH+2×MED，隔离探针实证，/tmp/review-results/v0382-adv.md）**：(adv-F1/c-F1 BLOCKER，探针实证) `pi.getAllTools()` 在扩展激活期**直接抛错**（pi 0.85 的 extension runtime 以 notInitialized 桩住全部 action 方法，`_bindExtensionCore` 要到所有扩展 factory 跑完才安装真实现）而 catch 静默吞掉 → toolIndex 永远为空 → 机械合并零贡献 → 46 个测试全绿（全注入已填充 index）却把 v0.3.78 adv-F1 静默丢失原样带回。修复：**注册整体延迟到首个 `session_start`**（registerSuperDevAgentsDeferred：运行时已绑定、全部 settings 包已激活、工具带真实 npm:<pkg> sourceInfo；15s unref 定时器兜底从不发该事件的宿主；每激活一次；构建失败响亮 WARN 后仍注册角色内建工具——胜过全部 delegation 报 Unknown agent）。(adv-F4/c-F2) “pi 按字母序加载扩展”的前提是错的：包加载顺序 = settings.json packages 数组序（pi-mcp-adapter(1)…pi-super-dev(15)→nowledge-mem-pi(16)→pi-blackhole(19)），本机 pi-blackhole/nowledge 在本扩展之后加载 — 激活期快照即便可调也不完整；延迟到 session_start 后快照是任意激活期视图的严格超集；懒注册工具（mcp__* 直连按服务器异步出现）结构性在任何一次性快照之外 — README 已明示用 all-tools 模式获取全集。(c-F3/adv-F5) all-tools 模式炸半径：未 pin 的 child 保留 ambient 扩展加载 → 会携带 ACTIVE super_dev 工具可递归嵌套 13 阶段流水线，且 WILDCARD 排除表漏 powershell（Windows 的 bash 孪生）— 修复：super_dev 对**所有角色**经 excludeTools 排除（递归守卫），powershell 同入只读/写者排除表。(adv-F2) 零贡献合并静默无声是构造性的 — 修复：声明包在 index 无条目时每包一次响亮 WARN（区分“纯 hook 无工具/懒注册/归属错配”）。(c-F4) allTools/agentAllTools 容器类型错误沿 v0.3.78 loud-fallback 契约每键一次 WARN（此前静默 false）。(adv-F3) 归属 regex 对 scoped 包截断（@scope/pkg 捕成 @scope）— buildToolIndexFromTools 重写为 scoped-aware 纯函数 + pi 包守卫修正（原守卫永不命中）。(adv-F6) 未测缝隙正是生产断裂处 — 补 buildToolIndexFromTools/buildToolIndex 单测（含抛错桩形状）、延迟注册四钉（arm 零注册/首触发注册/双触发 no-op/15s 兜底）、零贡献 WARN、malformed 告警。

findings（ownerStage=spec）直接路由 REPLAN（triggerReplanForFindings，复用既有机械）；共享文件耦合与 vitest coverage 工具缺失（run 14-14 七个 phase 的 UNMEASURABLE 根因）→ advisories 先行曝光。外部先例：PDoctor（arXiv:2404.17833 计划=约束可满足性）、Turborepo 2.9（校验你实际执行的图，加载时）。**A2 逃生阀 fail-open**：(a) judge 证据验证失败不再是静默 DISCARD（run 14-14 两次烧掉 no-progress 阀门）—— 一次纠错重呼把失败清单回灌 prompt（arXiv:2509.02761 critique-and-retry），二次仍失败 → escalate 保留诊断（F4 学说：诊断即产品；伪造纪律保留 —— 未验证裁决绝不按其声称路由，唯一地板是 escalate；受既有 per-signature/run 预算约束，预算耗尽才回落旧 discard）；(b) 执行期矛盾快断 —— phase 内 BLOCKING 边界回退计数（boundaryRevertHits）+ 相同失败签名 → impl-no-progress 阀门升级为矛盾帧（contradictionFastFailFrame，命名双 phase/回退文件/失败原因），开放 replan-upstream 路由，judge 裁决 replan-upstream → triggerReplanForFindings + 本轮停止（shouldIterateImplementation 门控再入）。**A3 停滞按发现类别路由**：评审基础设施未完成发现（"X review did not complete"）标记 infra（源点 failedReviewControl.infra + 标题后缀防御回退），永不计入停滞决策（spec-25 三轮 "3 recurring blockers" 全是该类，18 个修复周期白烧）；真实内容停滞停止前先一次有界 judge 裁决（stage10.stagnation.adjudicate，allowedRoutes=[replan-upstream]）—— 裁决 replan-upstream → REPLAN 而非人工 PARTIAL（修复循环做不了的设计级工作成为计划修订路由）；judge 失败/不可用保持今日人工停止（fail-open）。测试：tests/plan-feasibility.test.ts 12 项（含 run-14-14 逐形状夹具 + HEAD-导出可满足豁免 + 排序正确性）、tests/judge-evidence-failopen.test.ts 6 项、tests/verify-stagnation-infra.test.ts 5 项、tests/plan-contradiction-fastfail.test.ts 6 项（纯帧 + 源契约 wiring）；既有 judge 丢弃契约测试 8 项按新 fail-open 契约更新（其真实不变量 —— 未验证裁决绝不路由 —— 经 escalate 地板保留）；recordVerificationStagnation 转 async（3 个 await 调用点）。全套件 240 文件 3426 passed + 1 skipped，coverage 92.74%/81.2%/95.14%（85/80 门之上）。**双新鲜度评审修复**（sd-code-reviewer Changes Requested 1×P1+1×P2+3×P3；sd-adversarial-reviewer CONTEST 1×HIGH+4×MED，/tmp/review-results/v0379-*.md）：(CR-P1/adv 一致命) corrective 地板的 escalate 携带未验证 evidence fallback，"Never throws" 合同恢复）。(c-F2) agentExtensions/agentExtensionTools 同时接受裸角色键与 `sd-` 前缀键（pi 列表展示 sd- 形态，原为静默 no-op）。(nits) 缺包 WARN 措辞不再误归因首见 agent；docstring 如实注明 getConfig 吞解析错误 → DEFAULT_CONFIG（catch 为纵深防御）；README "file classifiers" 改为真实角色名（red-boundary/tdd-coverage）。

- **v0.3.77（stale model-exclusion 缓存分类为不可重试）** — 事故 2026-09-07T14-10-39-259Z：zai-coding-cn 5 小时配额窗口 15:11 封顶后，pi-subagents 的 model-exclusion registry 把 `glm-5.3`（15:11:49）与 `glm-5.3-flash`（15:12:36）各缓存为 24 小时排除（DEFAULT_MODEL_EXCLUSION_TTL_MS），且进程内永不重读。配额 19:05 重置后，父会话同一模型直连持续可用（pi core 从不查该 registry），而每个带显式 model pin 的 delegated child 在 `throwForExplicitModelExclusion` 处本地秒抛 `Requested subagent model '…' is excluded and cannot be replaced by a fallback`——重试同进程永不可能成功，但 reason 里的 `429` 字样诱导 TRANSIENT_RE 烧掉 4 次退避重试 × 3 轮 convergence（run.log :26-:93 实测）才到 round-3 FatalAbort。修复：ENVELOPE（`is excluded and cannot be replaced by a fallback`）而非缓存 reason 是签名——NON_RETRYABLE_AGENT_RE 新增该短语（runWithTransientRetry :371 本就先查不可重试，故同时消灭无谓重试与 3 轮烧毁，round-1 即 FatalAbort）；`nonRetryableAgentSummary` 对 exclusion 命中追加引擎-authored remedy（“stale in-process model-exclusion cache … Restart pi to clear it”）。附带证据：/tmp/pi-subagents-*/model-exclusions.json 磁盘文件被另一进程快照重写（lost-update）后已无 zai 条目——毒只在长驻进程内存里，重启即愈。上游问题（24h 平 TTL 无视 provider reset hint、registry 无进程内重读）另报。
  双新鲜度评审补丁（code-F2/adv-F2/F1）：①同类的 SIBLING 信封 `No usable subagent models remain after registry, scope, and cached-exclusion filtering`（未 pin/继承模型的调用把候选全部耗尽时的同源抛出）同样入 NON_RETRYABLE_AGENT_RE，remedy 同享；②remedy 补 auth/billing 形态的告诫（重启 alone 不清 auth.json 形态的缓存排除）；③修复 package-lock.json 根 version 误写为数字 1 的失手（版本对齐测试当场拦截）。
- **v0.3.76 (skill curation — 按调用裁剪 skill 注入，L0+L1+L3 + L2 采集)** — 测量动因：ambient 发现把全部 ~149 张卡片（~37K tokens）注入每个 delegated child 的系统提示（run 2026-09-07T00-49-33-204Z：99 个 agent 仅 1 次真实 skill 使用，task-classifier 以 50,914 input 完成 127 个输出 token，≥1.69M tokens 纯注意力稀释）。E2E 探针（scratch pi + 0.66.0）先证伪了父缝源码推断——per-call `skill:false` **不**压制 child 侧 ambient（child-launch.ts:298 `noSkills: !inheritSkills`：ambient 由**注册级** inheritSkills 门控，per-call skill 只控制附加的 curated 注入块），随后三点验证修正模型：classifier(inheritSkills:false)→NONE、research(false+skill:[firecrawl])→firecrawl、implementer(ambient 对照)→全量清单。落地：**L0** 机械分类器（task-classifier/judge/tdd-coverage/red-boundary）注册 inheritSkills:false；**L1** ClassificationData 新增可选 skillDomains（容忍字符串，仅已知域映射；域目录 map 驱动渲染进 buildClassifyPrompt），research-agent 拿 firecrawl 全家桶 ∪ 选中域成员；能力角色保持 ambient（2026-09-05 "subagents 与 pi 一致" 决定字面保留，仅凭 L2 实测数据收紧）；**L3** `SUPER_DEV_SKILLS=ambient` 一键恢复（注册级 inheritSkills 全 true + per-call 字段全缺省，opt-out 教义；与 SUPER_DEV_NO_SKILLS 注册级总开关正交）；**L2 采集** delegation 工具 tick（recentTools/currentTool）按调用去重落 `<specDir>/tool-usage.jsonl`（runId/agent/tool/argHead≤60 字符）——未来裁剪闭环的原始证据流，本波只采集不解读；config 新增 `agentSkills: Record<role, false | string[]>`（优先于内置分层）。tool-usage.jsonl 随即注册进 HARNESS_FILE_ROLES（events.jsonl 同权三角色——v0.3.75 review M1 教训的同类预防）。
**双审查修复**（sd-code-reviewer Changes Requested + sd-adversarial-reviewer CONTEST，同波合入，裁决表见 docs/plans/2026-09-07-v0.3.76-skill-curation.md §12）：(F1/R1+AR-2 BLOCKER) config agentSkills 此前只驱动 per-call 字段、注册门从不读 config —— 能力角色上 `false` 是静默 no-op、数组是 ambient+curated 双重注入（比不动更糟）；新增 explicitSkillConfigured：任何显式条目（含 []）同时翻转注册级 inheritSkills（ambient 压制只存在于注册级），注册层语义改动需重启 pi（文档已注明）；(F2/R3+AR-1) SUPER_DEV_SKILLS=ambient 改经 superDevEnv 解析（config env map 通道对 GUI 会话可达）并按会话 MEMOIZE —— 注册（activate 快照）与 per-call 读共享同一决策，中途翻转不再两层漂移；(F3/R2+AR-N3) skillsForCall 先过 skillsEnabled() —— SUPER_DEV_NO_SKILLS=1 恢复 pre-v0.3.76 全隔离合同（每层零卡），开关层级 NO_SKILLS > ambient > config > tiers；(F4/R4+AR-N2) 显式 `agentSkills: []` ≡ false（零卡）而非静默回落分层；(F5/AR-N1) file-classify 死条目移除（零调用点）；(AR-3 REFUTED) `request.skill` 并非 0.66 专属 —— npm pack 源码核验 0.64.0/0.65.1 的 supportedFields 同含 skill 且校验一致，无版本偏斜。
- **v0.3.75 (用量归因 dashboard — 用户 2026-09-07：run 结束后看到总 token/cost 与每 stage、每 agent 的拆分，优化才有方向)** — 证据动因：run 2026-09-07T00-49-33-204Z 里 task-classifier 以 50,914 input tokens 完成 127 个输出 token 的分类，~37K 的 ambient skill 清单开销在无归因时不可见。交付三件套（全部 best-effort、P5 永不惩罚 run）：(1) **per-call 账本** `<specDir>/usage-calls.jsonl` — 每次终端调用一行 JSON（id/agent/model/status/error/tokens/cache/cost/duration，runId 标记），失败调用即使无 usage 也记录（浪费的分发正是钱烧错的地方），per-call 追加 crash-durable，close-out 不重复 flush；(2) **`UsageAccumulator.byStage`** — 同一桶形按 stageKey(call.id) 聚合，轮次/尝试后缀（.aN/.tN/.rNN/.roundN）折叠、语义段（impl/red-review/runner-discovery/phase id）保留，重试聚合在一条 stage 行下；(3) **渲染 dashboard** `<specDir>/usage-report.md` + run.log 紧凑摘要 — 总览（含 cache-hit 占比）、按 cost 排序的 per-stage 表与 per-agent 表（sd- 前缀剥除展示）、Top-10 最贵单次调用、"fixed floor" 行（最便宜调用的 prompt 大小 ≈ skill 清单+工具+系统提示的固定开销底座，乘以调用数即 run 的固定成本）——为 v0.3.76 skill curation 提供前后对照的同一把尺子。Resume 诚实（P10）：报告只覆盖本次 pass，历史行留在账本里按 runId 可查。双 fresh-context 审查（sd-code-reviewer + sd-adversarial-reviewer）合并修正：(M1 BLOCKER，两位 reviewer 独立命中) `usage-calls.jsonl`/`usage-report.md` 注册进 HARNESS_FILE_ROLES（usage-calls.jsonl 对齐 events.jsonl 三角色、usage-report.md 对齐 completion-audit.md）——未注册时 merge-verify 会把自己追加的账本判 dirty、每个 merged run 降级 PARTIAL 且 .complete 永不写入，RED 边界还会把 per-call 引擎追加读成生产写入（v0.3.73 events.jsonl 事件同型）；(M2) realAgent catch 路径补落 failed 账本行——exec 抛出（如 owner listener 在 events.emit 同步抛）时"每次终端调用一行"的合同此前不成立，boundary 成功落行后的 quarantine throw 经 ledgerRowLanded 防双写；(M3) 行新增 dispatches 字段（transient 429/overload 重试计数，>1 才标记），fixed-floor 句改用 dispatch 总数；(M4) 报告头条改数终端行而非 usage-bearing 调用（all-failed run 不再读作 "0 agent call(s)"，附 failed/无 usage 计数），fixed-floor 只取有真实 prompt 侧测量值的行（completed-without-usage 不再伪造 "0 tokens" 底座）；(N1) USAGE_FIELDS 导出共享消灭三处内联副本；(N2) close-out 注释不再声称 flush 账本。
- **v0.3.74 (v0.3.73 run-audit 根因分层的设计波 — P1(a-d)+P2-e)** — 重分类结论：七个 M 缺陷没有一个是“修了就不复发”的纯实现 bug，四个坐在会再生产同类问题的设计缺口上，本波杀类不杀实例（根因分层表见 docs/findings/run-audit-2026-09-06.md）：(1) **harness 文件注册表（P1-a，M6 类）**：src/harness-paths.ts 成为所有 harness 记账/证据 basename 的唯一权威注册表（按消费者角色的 redBoundaryAnywhere / redBoundarySpecScoped / trackerAdvisoryNoise / internalRuntimeClaim 标志），tracking.ts 与 test-artifacts.ts 原来的四张平行 Set 字面量全部改为从注册表派生（golden-set + no-drift 源扫描测试）—— 新增记账文件只声明一次；(2) **task() 自动渲染兜底网（P1-b，M2 类）**：renderAndWrite 在入口把 stage id 标记到 SetupControl.renderedStageDocs（尝试级），task() 包装层在“该 stage 有渲染模型、本次运行从未渲染、返回的是非空对象”时自动渲染 —— 忘记手工 render 的新阶段不可能再留下五次完成零产物（手工每轮渲染仍权威；渲染验证器拒绝非法 control，垃圾结果不落盘）；(3) **超时档 env 旋钮（P1-c，M4 类）**：SUPER_DEV_CODE_TIMEOUT_MS / SUPER_DEV_REVIEW_TIMEOUT_MS / SUPER_DEV_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS 每次调用逐档覆盖（正有限数生效；垃圾值每变量 WARN 一次并保持档位默认）—— 换慢模型重新校准不再需要改代码 + 重载扩展；(4) **commit guard 子扩展（P2-e，M7 根，P4 机械预防）**：自包含零依赖的 src/child-guards/commit-guard.ts（child jiti 独立加载）注册 pi tool_call 钩子，对 implementer + tdd-guide 在工具层 BLOCK commit 类 git 调用（commit/merge/rebase/cherry-pick/stash/push；段级分类器容忍 flag 与链式，免疫 echo git commit / git log --grep=commit）；SUPER_DEV_NO_COMMIT_GUARD=1 逃生口；v0.3.73 HEAD 漂移检测器保留为 fail-open 侦探网；(5) **行为测试补钉（P1-d）**：M1 quarantine 裁决拯救（全归因即拯救、未声明路径保持 fail-open）与 M7 自提交检测器（advisory 且不阻断）在真实 tmp git repo 上获得完整 implementation-stage 行为测试，关闭 v0.3.73 双审查留下的“仅契约级证据”缺口。。
**双审查修复**（sd-code-reviewer Changes Requested + sd-adversarial-reviewer CONTEST，同波合入，裁决表见 docs/findings/run-audit-2026-09-06.md）：(F1 BLOCKER) guard 扩展路径 ./child-guards 误解析到 src/agents/child-guards（不存在）——CLI spawn 形态子进程直接退出、in-process 形态静默丢失，而注册测试的子串断言是假绿；改为 ../child-guards + existsSync 校验 + WARN-once 缺失降级，注册测试断言路径真实存在；(F2 MAJOR) `extensions` 字段会禁用子进程的 ambient 扩展发现（child-tool-plan.ts:403），恰与"子代理与 pi 本体一致"的用户决策冲突 —— guard 改走 `subagentOnlyExtensions`（child-only 加载，ambient 完整保留）；(F3 MED×2) 注册表补齐 helpers.ts 第五张与 implementation.ts 第六张平行字面量（specDirBookkeeping 十名 + phaseCommitExcluded 两名，.convergence-ledger.json 新入册），no-drift 扫描扩到全部四个消费文件；(CR-F2 MED) 分类器误伤只读形态 —— git merge-base / stash list/show/clear 不再拦截（`(?![\w-])` 终结符 + stash 负向前瞻），并补齐 revert/pull/am 与带引号动词（git "commit"）；(AR-F6 MIN) 超时旋钮增加 1 秒下限（秒数误填毫秒按垃圾值大声回退）。
- **v0.3.73（真实运行审计修复）** — run 2026-09-05T23-09-55-596Z（v0.3.72 首个真实任务，11h03m，9/9 phase 全绿）全量日志审计的七个类级修复（docs/findings/run-audit-2026-09-06.md）：(1) **M1 red-review 裁决拯救**：并发写者 quarantine 不再丢弃已形成的裁决 — workflow 把 control 附加到 boundary 错误（salvagedControl），join 用新纯分类器 attributeQuarantinePaths 判定"全部违规路径都是 implementer 声明文件"时恢复裁决正常裁决（事故 6 次 54 分钟全浪费，路径 100% 是 implementer 的文件）；(2) **M2 tests-review 渲染**：verify testsReview 任务补 renderAndWrite + STAGE_MODELS.testsReview（5 次完成 0 落盘）；(3) **M3 close-on-success**：convergence-ledger 新增 closeAgentFailedFindings — 后续轮次成功控制关闭 `${kind}-agent-failed` 行（事故审计把已 APPROVED 的 code review 仍列为 open·blocking）；(4) **M4 reviewer 30min 档**：六个 reviewer 角色进 30 分钟超时档（事故 7 次精确 20:00 超时）；(5) **M5 全局 ledger 防污染**：SUPER_DEV_NO_GLOBAL_METRICS=1 守卫 + vitest hermeticity setup 全局设置 + 一次性清理 481→1 行（σ-band 基线被测试行灌成 median=0 → 每个真实运行假 3σ）；(6) **M6 spec 作用域记账豁免**：events.jsonl 等 8 个 run 记账 basename 仅在 …/specifications/ 下豁免（位置感知，同 isHarnessBookkeepingPath），RED 不再被 run 自己的 events.jsonl 追加误判 red-polluted；(7) **M7 self-commit 禁令**：implementer/tdd-guide 提示词加 "commits are engine-owned" 规则 + 引擎 HEAD 漂移检测（advisory WARN + low finding，事故 2e92da3/5d4790d 两次自提交）。 **双审查修复**（同波合入，merged verdict 见 docs/findings/run-audit-2026-09-06.md）：M1 salvage 谓词改为仅 implementer 声明文件（testFiles 无条件声明不再作为 salvage 证据，restore 语义不变）；M6 豁免锚定 docs/specifications/ 前缀（任意同名段不再豁免）；closeAgentFailedFindings 自持久化（崩溃窗口不再复活幻影行）+ 闭注为引擎常量（无 agent 文本入 duty 前缀字段）；M5 守卫走 superDevEnv seam（支持 config 持久关闭）+ 非 vitest 环境设置时 WARN 一次；M7 检测扩到 tdd-guide 窗口且全部 rev-parse 带 5s timeout；M2 源契约测试改精确调用锚定（原切片是假绿）。
- **v0.3.72 (双审查员复审修复)** — fresh-context code + adversarial 双审查对 v0.3.63–v0.3.71 十个未推 commit 的合并裁决 CONTEST→修复：(1) **usage 计量全准确（M1）**：corrective 轮与瞬态重试的每一次尝试都烧真 token，`mergeUsage`（types.ts）逐字段求和后线程化进 delegation-backend 的全部 return 位（含非完成终端），`runWithTransientRetry` 把所有尝试合并进这一个逻辑调用的结果 — 保险丝与 run-metrics 不再系统性少计；(2) **版本偏斜 shape B（M2）**：pi-subagents 自身 import 链的 `Cannot find module '…/pi-subagents/…'`（2026-09-05 detached runner 实测形态）与 `Failed to load extension` 同类：均进 sticky fail-fast + Restart pi 补救，路径必须含 pi-subagents 以免误伤；(3) **fuse 非数值不再静默失效（M3）**：set-but-unparseable 的 SUPER_DEV_MAX_RUN_COST/TOKENS 按变量 WARN 一次（loud-unlimited，非假上限、非误杀）；(4) **model-not-found 入 NON_RETRYABLE（N1）**：`Unknown subagent model / Model X not found / no such model` 即刻 FatalAbort（省 3 轮/循环）；(5) **STRUCTURED_UNSUPPORTED_RE 收紧（N2）**：必须命名 delegation field，裸词 structured 不再永久降级保真度；(6) register-agents 部分注册文案修正（M4）；M5（deliverablesAlreadyMet notContains 语义）带证据 CLEAR，N3（sigma-band 咨询性噪声）记录不动。裁决与证据全文见 docs/findings/external-review-2026-09-05.md。
- **v0.3.71 (W4 review policy caps F10-4 + plan Risks/Proof F10-7)** — review outputs feed writer re-prompt rounds, so verbose low-signal findings inflate every downstream round (SDLC REVIEW.md play: "cap the nits"; OpenAI guidance: concise high-signal). (1) Both reviewer prompts (`agents/code-reviewer.md`, `agents/adversarial-reviewer.md`) gain a **Finding Discipline** section: Important-vs-Nit definitions (Important = correctness/security/data-loss/performance/contract breaks/untested changed behavior; Nit = style, naming, preference, non-idiomatic-but-correct), a nit cap of at most 3 with nits SUPPRESSED entirely when any blocking finding exists, a do-not-report list (formatting/lint-covered, hypotheticals without evidence, tests for unchanged behavior, TODOs unless risk-related, restating what the code does), and output discipline (file:line + severity + one-line suggested fix; no code restatement). (2) Plans gain per-phase Risks (concrete failure modes: concurrency, data loss, contract breaks, known escape classes) and Proof (how completion is proven: deterministic gate / test command / deliverable clause) — `SpecPhase.risks`/`.proof` are OPTIONAL TypeBox arrays (old specs and resume replays valid without them; template renders the sections only when present), the spec-writer prompt instructs at most 3 one-liners each and to omit when nothing material (never pad). Tests: +6 source-contract tests (prompt discipline regexes for both reviewers, SpecPhase schema acceptance/rejection incl. non-array rejection, template renders with and without the fields, spec-writer bounded instruction); suite 3238 passed + 1 skipped, tsc clean.

- **v0.3.70 (W3 structured delegation, plan §5 / decision D7 Option C)** — F10-6 root cause: `call.schema` flowed into the delegation options but the backend hardcoded `result:{kind:"text"}` — the schema never reached the child, prose was re-parsed, and the only engine-side check was missing-key names (a wrong-TYPED field burned a full corrective round with no signal). This wave completes the link: (1) **wire** — schema-carrying calls now send `result:{kind:"structured",schema}` so the child gains a `structured_output` tool validated at call time in its own conversation (pi-subagents 0.65 subagent-prompt-runtime.ts:420 — an invalid value is repaired in-turn, not by burning a delegation round); (2) **engine stays authoritative (P5)** — structured values AND parsed prose controls are re-validated with TypeBox `Value.Errors` (`typebox/value`, bundled-aliased by pi's extension loader; verified in dev via the repo devDependency) and the corrective re-prompt now carries detailed JSON-pointer violations (`/verdict: must be equal to one of the allowed values`) instead of only missing-key names — the industry validate→repair pattern, still bounded to one corrective round; a retried control that still violates returns an honest schema-violation error (P10) instead of a silently wrong-typed control; (3) **never fatal (P4)** — two automatic sticky per-process degrades to text mode (schema rides the prompt; engine validation unchanged): an invalid_request naming the structured fields (in-memory 0.64 owner after `pi update`; the CALL itself retries in text mode and succeeds) and 3 consecutive `structured_output_failed` terminals; each WARNs once via the progress log; (4) **escape hatch** — `SUPER_DEV_STRUCTURED=0` opts out entirely (default ON per Option C; scheduled for removal two versions after stabilization); (5) **STAGE_MODELS audit (§5.2.5)** — every `controlKeys` call site now carries a schema: new `JudgeControlData` (the judge `route` union — a free-text route silently fell to escalate-now; it is now a correctable violation), `TddCoverageControlData`, `FileClassifyControlData` wired at implementation/verify call sites, with a P6 dynamic cross-check test pinning the route union against `stages/judge.ts JUDGE_ROUTES` and a source-scan test that no controlKeys call site lacks a schema. **E2E verified live**: real pi-subagents 0.65 owner + real zai glm-5.3 child — the child called `structured_output` (turns=1, tools=1), the structured value arrived schema-valid, became the control directly with zero corrective rounds, and usage threaded into the result (v0.3.68 chain intact). Tests: +12 in tests/structured-delegation.test.ts (wire/text-unchanged/escape hatch, structured-response acceptance, corrective violation feedback + convergence + honest still-violating error, both sticky degrades with WARN-once, streak reset, prose-mode validation, typebox spike, judge-union cross-check, controlKeys-coverage source scan); suite 3232 passed + 1 skipped, tsc clean.

- **v0.3.69 (auto-continuous evolution loop, E-wave)** — plan §9 (docs/plans/2026-09-05-v0.3.68-hardening-plan.md): the fix lifecycle (findings → class tests → version) was already the governed half of the 2026 self-harness pattern (Weng "Harness Engineering", Self-Harness arXiv 2606.09498, Arize governance); this wave automates the missing MEASURE–DETECT–DIAGNOSE half. **E1 σ-band monitor**: every run now ALSO appends its metrics row to the global `<super-dev-dir>/run-metrics.jsonl`; at close-out median+MAD robust bands classify the run per metric (wallMs, costUsd, tokens, agentErrorRounds, fatalAborts, agentsSpawned) — ≥8 prior runs before banding (honest insufficientHistory below), MAD=0 → equal is 0σ / any deviation capped 3σ, 1σ logged / 2σ post-mortem flag / 3σ outlier surfaced; deterministic, zero-LLM, never throws. **E2 post-mortem agent**: new read-only `sd-post-mortem` (agents/post-mortem.md) reads run artifacts just-in-time (paths in the task, never full logs), matches failures to the P1–P10 escape classes, and returns a STRUCTURED finding draft; the ENGINE validates (fail-closed: title, ≥1 quoted evidence, hypothesis, prediction mandatory) and writes `docs/findings/inbox/` — the agent can never write (P4: the boundary is mechanical). Auto-invoked at run end only when `postMortem: "auto"` in config (default manual) and status != success. **E3 triage**: `npm run triage` — zero-LLM human Decide gate; `--list` drafts, `--approve` moves them to docs/findings/ with an approved header (fix lifecycle takes over); refuses overwrites. **E4 evals**: `npm run evals` = the incident-pinned behavioral suites (`v0.3.*` test names) — the model-change gate to run after agentModels/agentThinking edits. **E5 prediction ledger**: finding drafts carry `prediction: <metric> <direction>`; the close-out checker compares the last-3-run median against the prior baseline (n≥3 each) and appends supported/refuted verdicts — never overwriting an earlier verdict, unverified stays unverified. Explicitly NOT adopted (plan §9.4): ungated self-modification, semantic/embedding clustering, auto-applying prompt edits. Tests: +15 (global ledger isolation, band tiers incl. MAD=0 and junk rows, gating, draft validation/render/write, engine-writes-not-agent delegation pair, triage --list/--approve + overwrite refusal, prediction supported/refuted/unverified/no-overwrite); suite 3220 passed + 1 skipped.

- **v0.3.68 (usage governance + run-metrics harvest)** — external review F10-1/F10-2 (docs/findings/external-review-2026-09-05.md; plan docs/plans/2026-09-05-v0.3.68-hardening-plan.md, decision D6 方案 A): delegated usage was parsed for one terminal log line and dropped — no aggregation, no RunSummary cost block, and the only budget fuse counted SPAWNS while tokens inside each spawn were unbounded (Anthropic: multi-agent ≈ 15× chat tokens). (1) **Accounting**: `SpawnResult.usage` (threaded from the delegation terminal response at all four return sites), a run-scoped `UsageAccumulator` (`ctx.usage` — totals + per-`sd-*`-agent split), and a `RunSummary.usage` block; absent usage is never fabricated (P10). (2) **Fuses (fail-closed 方案 A)**: `SUPER_DEV_MAX_RUN_COST` (USD) / `SUPER_DEV_MAX_RUN_TOKENS` (input+output) checked BEFORE each call — the call that lands at/over the cap still completes and counts honestly, the NEXT call fails closed naming the fuse and spent/limit numbers; tripped rows ride the existing v0.3.65 agent-error machinery (FatalAbort after 3 consecutive) so a blown budget winds down deterministically with zero further spend while close-out (summary/audit/metrics) still runs and the run stays resume-safe (raise the fuse, resume) — the reasons this beat a hard abort. (3) **Harvest**: `<specDir>/run-metrics.jsonl` — one deterministic JSON row per run (status, agentsSpawned, wallMs, stage histogram, `agentErrorRounds`, `fatalAborts`, usage totals, ts; best-effort, never throws) — the closing-the-loop feed the σ-band monitor (v0.3.69 E-wave) reads instead of hand-mining prose logs. Tests: +12 (usage threading/accumulation/summary, both fuses incl. at-cap semantics and no-fabrication, metrics row shape + append durability + wiring source contracts); rejected-by-review F10-3/F10-5 (effort tiers, per-role inheritSkills) were dropped with their tests per plan D2/D3. Suite 3196 passed + 1 skipped.

### Fixed
- **v0.3.67 (no-op completions, honest unknown reasons, REPLAN wind-down)** — incident 2026-09-04T14-45-04-784Z second half (attempts 3-10): with the phase deliverables verifiably satisfied on disk, Stage 9 still burned ~10h across three coupled defects. (A) pi-subagents' child-acceptance layer (MISSING_IMPLEMENTATION_MUTATION_MESSAGE + LLM intent arbiter, "an agent that made no edits may still have failed to implement") rejected tdd-guide's legitimate verification-only completions — 21 rejected calls each discarded the control, blanked testFiles, and looped `red-unverified` retries; the fix routes a no-edit rejection through the deterministic live deliverable re-check into the EXISTING Already-satisfied verification node (build gate + deliverable check re-run live; A1: the machine decides, never the child's summary; fail-closed when deliverables are not satisfied). (B) P10: when the RED oracle was unknown because the agent died/was rejected, `redEvidenceFailureReasons` and `redGenerationRetryHint` printed the canned "no supported test runner was available" even though R1 had recorded the true cause in `reason` — a false environment-defect claim that misdirected agents (runner re-verification loops) and judges (harness-source archaeology) for hours; both formatters now LEAD with the overridden reason, keeping the runner guidance only for genuine no-runner evidence. (C) a REPLAN round routed at 11:24 executed only at the 18:56 restart — in between the §D loop re-attempted Stage 9 four more times (~4h) on phases a judge had proven unsatisfiable and Stage 10 ran 3 review cycles (~2h) against the invalidated spec; the pass now WINDS DOWN (Fox/Gerevini/Long/Serina ICAPS-06 plan stability; Nav2 #1395 replan-immediately-on-invalidation; GitHub Actions cancel-in-progress, stage-aware): `replanPending()` gates the phases loop (deferred, named log), the §D `while` predicate, and the Stage 10 branch (named skip notice; in-flight phases break naturally, partial-preserve untouched). Tests: +14 (formatter honesty pins, no-edit escape + fail-closed negative, replanPending/§D/Stage-10 predicates, notice log, phases-deferral no-op); suite 3184 passed + 1 skipped.
- **v0.3.66 (already-satisfied escape reachable for every contract shape)** — incident 2026-09-04T14-45-04-784Z (pi-omisis spec-24, phase 5 macro-registry-amendment): with the phase's ENTIRE contains-only deliverable contract verifiably satisfied on disk (schemas.ts `"macro"` from dbdeb16 06:21, CLOSED_THIRTEEN/toBe(13) from 962ea41 07:30, `"macro": "fetcher-bridge"` from bf0687d 09:16), the RED loop still spun 16× `red-not-confirmed: tests passed before implementation` across 7 RED tries × 3 stage re-entries plus one no-progress escalation (~3.5h) — a satisfiable phase that could never converge. Two coupled root causes: (1) `deliverablesAlreadyMet` (src/build-runner/gates.ts) returned false whenever `requireFiles` was empty/absent regardless of requireContains/requireNotContains/requireScenarios satisfaction — a guard predating the contains/scenarios clauses, while sibling `runDeliverableCheck` already evaluated every shape (two grammars for one contract, escape class A+D); (2) the classifier consumed the attempt-ENTRY baseline at oracle time, so deliverables landing between entry and oracle (sibling commits, RED-authored test-file deliverables) were invisible — the entry re-check then re-computed the same false baseline forever (RED cleanup erased the authored deliverables each attempt). Fix (class-level): `deliverablesAlreadyMet` now evaluates ALL clause kinds and requires ≥1 checkable clause (empty contracts stay fail-closed); the RED classifier re-checks the contract LIVE at oracle time when the oracle is green and the baseline was false, with an honest flip log line; classification order unchanged (`polluted-red` first — a RED-phase production edit can never masquerade) and the Already-satisfied verification node still re-runs build gate + deliverable check deterministically (A1: no agent self-report). The resume no-op fast path inherits the clause coverage. Tests (18 new): a 13-row contract-shape enumeration table on real fs (tests/deliverables-already-met.test.ts) + incident-class hermetic integration with misroute guard and pollution-order pins (tests/implementation-red-already-satisfied.test.ts).
- **v0.3.65 (agent-error review rounds never masquerade as verdicts)** — incident 2026-09-04T13-45-10-739Z (pi-omisis spec-24, super-dev v0.3.62 serving from a pre-fix pi session): the second pi-subagents version-skew episode killed every design/designReview child in <250 ms, and the convergence loop counted each dead reviewer as a "review rejected" round — 16 of 18 design rounds were fake (budget auto-extended 8→24, ~10 min burned on cached error replays) because a reviewer AGENT error was indistinguishable from a genuine verdict. Root cause (file:line): writerTask's G21 path (src/nodes.ts) deliberately records an honest failed row and returns ok + EMPTY control so convergence retry feedback runs — but `task()` returns `{status:"ok"}` to the loop, so the loops' `reviewResult.status === "failed"` branches were unreachable for agent errors and the empty control fell through to the verdict extraction → "review rejected" (the writer side fell to the validators, masking the infra cause with generic schema messages). Fix (P5/P7 class-level, single mechanism at the common ancestor): G21 rows now carry `cause:"agent-error"` (StageResult field), `agentErrorTextsSince` makes them visible at every convergence decision point, and BOTH loops (artifact-convergence for requirements/bdd/design writer+review sites, spec-convergence for spec writer + specReview sites) label agent-error rounds honestly (`✗ review agent errored round N (k/3 consecutive)`), keep G21's two-round transient tolerance, and FatalAbort with the INFRA ERROR NAMED (`… errored 3 consecutive round(s) — infra failure, not an artifact defect (last error: …)`) on the third consecutive FRESH round; non-retryable errors (isNonRetryableAgentError) still abort immediately on round 1, and replayed rounds (`round ≤ priorRounds`, the AC-17 convention) never count so a resumed run with a fixed runtime recovers. Tests (7 new): 3-consecutive review/writer fatals with message + bounded round counts + no-masquerade log assertions, interleaved-errors converge (counter resets), non-retryable round-1 abort, replay-guard, and the two spec-site wirings.

### Changed
- **v0.3.64 (single specialist backend: pi-subagents delegation; session + subprocess backends removed)** — after verifying live (2026-09-04, scratch installs of both pi-subagents 0.64 and 0.65) that per-agent `extensions` in the sd-* registration load pi-web-access/pi-mcp-adapter/pi-browser-cdp-extension inside delegated children on BOTH architectures (0.64 spawns `pi` CLI children with `-e`; 0.65 runs in-process children with explicit extension paths), the last capability gap between the three backends closed and the architecture collapsed to one execution path:
  - **pi-subagents is now a HARD requirement.** Without an owner in the process, every specialist call fails CLOSED with the `pi install pi-subagents` instruction (activation logs the same ERROR; README gained a Requirements section). The old whole-run degrade to `session` is gone — v0.3.26's hang prevention is preserved, v0.3.64 stops pretending there is a second engine.
  - **Browser (qa-agent, ui-tester) and web-research (research-agent) agents delegate like every other role** — their tool extensions ride the registration's per-agent `extensions` (agent-runtime.extensionsForAgent resolves package `pi.extensions` manifests to entry paths; missing packages degrade to an empty list). Declaring extensions disables AMBIENT discovery for that child, preserving the isolation these roles always had.
  - **Version-skew (v0.3.63 class) adapts to fail-closed**: the sticky flag now makes later calls fail FAST with the restart remedy instead of degrading to a session backend that no longer exists.
  - `Unknown agent` answers surface as the call's error naming the re-register remedy (per-call, NOT sticky) instead of degrading.
  - **Reflection delegates too**: runReflection threads the extension's event bus and runs sd-reflection through the same machinery (visible in Fleet); without a bus (bench/tests) it skips with a NAMED audit row (P10) — AuditEntry gained an optional `skipped` field.
  - **Deleted**: `src/pi-spawn.ts` (subprocess spawner, RPC driver wiring, ~1455 lines), `src/rpc-driver.ts`, `src/subprocess-structured-output.ts`, `src/session-agent.ts`'s pipeline role (the session executor survives VERBATIM as `src/bench/session-agent.ts`, the standalone convergence bench's dev-harness caller — documented bench-only, never imported by the pipeline). Shared utilities (model/thinking resolution chains, role classification, extension-package resolution, timeout tiers, skills switch, slug summarization, tool-call summaries, SessionModelOption/ThinkingLevel types) moved to `src/agents/agent-runtime.ts`.
  - **Config surface removed**: `agentBackend` config key, `SUPER_DEV_BACKEND` env, `RunOptions.backend`, and the tool's backend selection (the `backend` tool parameter is still ACCEPTED but ignored, so legacy callers are not rejected). `SUPER_DEV_NO_RPC_SPAWN` is gone with the RPC spawner.
  - Tests: 6 subprocess/session-machinery suites deleted, the session suite repointed at the bench module, shared-utility coverage migrated to `tests/agent-runtime.test.ts`, ~15 workflow-level suites retargeted from session/subprocess mocks to the delegation seam (with owner-present + event-bus fixtures), and `workflow-delegation-backend.test.ts` rewritten as the delegation-only behavioral spec (fail-closed classes, sticky skew, per-role delegation, Fleet wrap). Structure test now pins the single-backend architecture invariant.

### Fixed
- **v0.3.63 (pi-subagents 0.65.0 version-skew degrade)** — incident 2026-09-04 14:56 (pi-omisis spec-24, `agentBackend: pi-subagents`): `pi update` auto-upgraded pi-subagents 0.64.0→0.65.0 (published 04:51 UTC) at 13:56 UNDER the live pi session, and the next super-dev run lost EVERY specialist of two stages (~5 s each) to `Failed to load extension "…pi-subagents…/subagent-prompt-runtime.ts": … Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'runtimeAcknowledgements')`. Root cause is a version skew, not a contract break: 0.65 replaced the spawned-`pi`-CLI child architecture with native in-process `AgentSession`s (#1844) and changed `subagent-prompt-runtime.ts`'s activate signature from `(pi)` (env-driven, 0.64 line 689) to `(pi, config)` whose first statement reads `config.runtimeAcknowledgements` (0.65 line 445) — pi's loader calls a file-loaded extension with exactly one argument, so the file is now loadable ONLY as an inline config-carrying factory (0.65 filters it out of `-e` paths at child-launch.ts:272). The parent session still held 0.64's bridge IN MEMORY, whose spawn protocol keeps passing the path unfiltered (`pi-args.ts:579`) — each child loaded the on-disk 0.65 file, crashed at startup, 0.64's startup retry burned ~5 s, and the error surfaced as the delegation response. Verified end-to-end in a scratch agent dir: (a) fresh 0.65 + runtime-agent registration + our exact delegation payload → `status=completed` (our v0.3.25 event contracts are 0.65-compatible unchanged); (b) `pi -p "Say OK" -e <0.65 prompt-runtime.ts>` reproduces the production error byte-identically. Fix (P5 — executor infra failure never punishes the work): `isDelegationRuntimeExtensionFailure` (src/agents/delegation-backend.ts, package-scoped `Failed to load extension "…pi-subagents…"` signature) triggers a per-call degrade to the session backend AND arms a sticky whole-process degrade so later calls skip delegation entirely (no per-call 5 s burn), with one actionable WARN naming the remedy (restart pi / re-align the package). Tests pin the classifier (production byte shape, any pi-subagents-internal path, non-pi-subagents extension failures and ordinary errors rejected) and the degrade wiring (degrade-on-first-signature, sticky skip, out-of-package failures stay visible).

### Added
- **v0.3.30 (JVM/Gradle/Maven oracle + universal structured classification + agent-proposed runner discovery)** — run 2026-08-28T16-09-12-785Z (AnkiQuick) burned 6 RED tries / 28 min entirely on harness gaps: the deterministic red-oracle had ZERO JVM awareness (no Gradle/Maven branch anywhere — `detectProjectCommands` fell through to `mixed`, zero test plans, `unknown` every try while the agent's own gradle proved 127 tests ran / 122 failed); the fail-closed guard relabeled `unknown` to `broken-test` so logs lied "tests did not compile/collect"; a judge `fix-environment` verdict restarted RED with a hint that sent the agent full-disk hunting the harness's own source (scope escape); and spec-dir `.judge.jsonl` (judge audit) polluted the RED boundary as a "production file". Fixes, designed from a deep survey of how other harnesses verify tests (aider = user-configured `--test-cmd` + exit codes only; codex/superpowers = the agent eyeballs output; swebench = curated per-repo specs; RepoLaunch/SWE-Builder = **LLM proposes the runner under a mandatory per-test-evidence contract, the harness machine-verifies and caches**; flake/GitLab/Harness CI = **JUnit XML/TAP as the universal interchange**):
  - **F1 JVM support**: `detectProjectCommands` recognizes Gradle (`build.gradle(.kts)`/`settings.gradle(.kts)`, Android detection reads root + first-level module files → `./gradlew testDebugUnitTest` / `compileDebugSources`, pure JVM → `test`/`classes`) and Maven (`pom.xml` → `mvn test`); `PROJECT_MANIFEST_NAMES` grows the JVM manifests; `gradleRedCheckPlans`/`mavenRedCheckPlans` derive per-test-class FQNs from the `src/(android)test/(java|kotlin)` layout, target the OWNING module with the root wrapper, and scope with `--tests`/`-Dtest=`; `classifyRedStatus` gains the JVM branch including a **greenfield heuristic** (compile failed with ONLY `unresolved reference`/`cannot find symbol` errors → the textbook greenfield RED for compiled languages — also kills the run 12-51-40 trap).
  - **F2 honest unknown**: `unknown-*` evidence keeps its status (own `red-unverified` reason + retry-hint templates that scope repairs INSIDE the worktree and forbid production-code workarounds) instead of being coerced to `broken-test`; the post-loop terminal gate only fails on unknown when the fail-closed guard engaged, preserving the documented "proceed without stalling" contract for phases that don't require tests.
  - **F3 fix-environment containment**: `MAX_RED_ENV_RESTARTS` (env `SUPER_DEV_MAX_RED_ENV_RESTARTS`, default 1) grants ONE restart for genuinely in-repo environment repairs; the next `fix-environment` verdict terminates with `environment-blocked`; the restart hint now scopes repairs to this worktree and tells the agent to STOP rather than hunt external files when the fix is harness-side.
  - **F4**: `.judge.jsonl` joins `RUNTIME_EVIDENCE_BASENAMES` so judge audit appends between RED tries are boundary noise no more.
  - **Layer A (universal structured classification)**: new `src/build-runner/result-parse.ts` — JUnit XML counts (testsuite/testsuites aggregation), TAP counts, and `classifyFromStructuredCounts` (failures>0 → red; errors>0 → broken; zero tests + failing exit → broken; clean + exit 0 → green). `runRedCheck` now harvests fresh JUnit XML from conventional result dirs (`build/test-results/**` incl. Gradle variant dirs, `target/surefire-reports|failsafe-reports/`, mtime-bounded to this invocation) and classifies from STRUCTURED counts BEFORE any console regex — language-agnostic ground truth; console regexes stay as fallback.
  - **Layer C (agent-proposed runner, machine-verified, cached)**: new `src/build-runner/runner-discovery.ts` — when the registry knows nothing about a stack, ONE `runner-discovery` agent call (RepoLaunch's mandatory contract: the command MUST emit per-test pass/fail detail) proposes a command; the harness EXECUTES it and validates parseable evidence (JUnit XML harvest or TAP); a validated spec is cached to the spec dir (`test-runner.json`, boundary-excluded) and threads into every later `runRedCheck` via `opts.runner` → `dynamicRedCheckPlans`. LLM proposes, machine verifies — the gate decision stays deterministic, and a new stack costs one discovery call per repo, ever.
- **v0.3.28 (full-field progress parity across all three backends)** — run.log under `agentBackend: pi-subagents` had degraded to bare tool names (`requirements-clarifier: ls`): `onUpdate` read only `currentTool` from pi-subagents' `SubagentDelegationUpdate`, discarding `currentToolArgs`, `recentOutputLines`, and the terminal `usage` (the reference consumers pi-prompt-template-model and pi-astro surface all of them). All three backends now log uniformly: tool lines `label: → tool args…` (the delegation backend diffs the append-only `recentTools` history so tools that finish between ticks still surface; a per-attempt set dedupes rapid identical ticks), narration lines `label: ⇢ <text>` (session-backend narration previously fed only the TUI typing effect and never landed in run.log; the subprocess backend logged it unprefixed), and a terminal summary per call — `<backend> <label>: completed status=… model=… turns=N tools=N tokens=in/out cache=r/w $cost duration=Xs` via the shared `agentTerminalLine` (`src/progress-lines.ts`). The usage data was always available but never aggregated: the delegation terminal response carries `usage`; a session's assistant messages carry `{input, output, cacheRead, cacheWrite, cost:{total}}` + model (verified against a live sd-* child session.jsonl); the subprocess child's `message_end` events carry the same — `RpcDriver` now accumulates them (message_end is intercepted before `onRawEvent`, which is also now surfaced to callers after internal capture so the final narration block flushes) and both subprocess loops (rpc + json) emit the summary on success. Lifecycle lines too: `delegation label: request agent=sd-… timeout=…ms cwd=…` mirrors the session/subprocess start lines.
- **v0.3.27 (Fleet visibility — external runs actually appear)** — root cause of "Fleet shows no turns/tools/tokens" during run 2026-08-28T16-09-12: external-run records were registered with `sessionManager.getSessionId()` (a bare uuid on pi 0.84.3) while pi-subagents' Fleet filters by `resolveCurrentSessionId()` = `getSessionFile() ?? getSessionId()` (the session FILE path), so `snapshotExternalRuns` returned 0 rows for our records in every session — the registration and module loading themselves were fine (verified with an in-process probe: bare-specifier import fails under pi's jiti from git-installed extensions, but the absolute-path dynamic import our resolver uses loads fine). `resolvePiSessionIdentity()` now mirrors the exact precedence. README documents the viewing model: Fleet (`Ctrl+Alt+F`) must be opened in the pi session that started the run (foreground state is per-process); under the pi-subagents backend each call is a real foreground run with turns/tools/tokens and a live transcript under `<session-dir>/subagent-artifacts/`; the “Async Result” panel is async-only. Upstream note: pi-subagents' own `docs/extension-api.md` example has the same uuid-vs-file-path bug under this pi version.
- **v0.3.26 (delegation fail-safes)** — the pi-subagents backend can no longer take a run down with it. Root cause of run 2026-08-28T15-50-08 (8 requirements rounds burned in ~2.5 s on instant `Unknown agent: sd-*` errors): pi-subagents' runtime-agent validator rejects system prompts with leading/trailing whitespace, and raw `agents/*.md` bodies end with a trailing newline — only the 2 files that happened to end without one registered, so 27 of 29 specialists were unresolvable. Three guarantees now hold: (1) `loadAgentBasePrompt` trims (all 29 registrations pass the strict validator, pinned by a test that mirrors `validateString`); (2) the activation registration handshake doubles as a capability probe (`delegationOwnerPresent()`) — with no pi-subagents owner in the process the WHOLE backend degrades to `session` with one actionable WARN (previously every call would hang to the 20-minute timeout backstop), and registration outcomes are summarized loudly (`registered N/29`, ERROR lines for rejections) instead of silently logged; (3) a per-call `Unknown agent` answer degrades that single call to `session` instead of letting a fast total failure consume convergence rounds. Install-time dependency checking is deliberately NOT auto-install: the delegation contract requires pi-subagents loaded by pi itself (an npm dep would be a different module instance on the bus), so the extension detects-and-instructs instead.
- **v0.3.25 (pi-subagents integration — same subagent machinery as pi itself)** — three layers, all zero-runtime-import against pi-subagents@0.58 event contracts: L1 FleetView visibility (`src/agents/fleet-visibility.ts`, always-on in extension mode) publishes a display-only external run per specialist call (register → throttled live `currentAction` → terminal state/preview) through the `pi-subagents.external-runs` registry, resolved lazily from the absolute install path so the module instance is shared with pi; L2 structured-delegation backend (`src/agents/delegation-backend.ts`, `backend: "pi-subagents"` via the tool param > config `agentBackend` > `SUPER_DEV_BACKEND`) executes every specialist call through pi-subagents' delegation executor — Fleet-UI rows with real turns/tools/tokens, live steering, `<control>` parsing byte-identical via `extractControl` (review-2 P0: unwraps the real `{kind:"text",text}` result envelope), one corrective re-prompt (new requestId, same nodeId) honoring `allowEmptyArraysFor` + the `filesCreated/Modified/Deleted` allow-set, pre-abort bail before emitting, `defaultAgentTimeoutMs` backstop, `on()`-return listener detach, inherited model/thinking resolution identical to the other backends, cancel event on timeout/abort, graceful degradation to `session` without an event bus; L3 runtime agent registration (`src/agents/register-agents.ts`) registers all 29 specialists as `sd-*` first-class pi agents at activation using the base `agents/*.md` prompt (learned lessons stay per-call — `loadAgentBasePrompt`), read-only vs coding tool split, including the four design specialists. Fleet wrap + delegation are tested at both unit and workflow level.
- **v0.3.20 (modern language profiles)** — the five `agents/lang/*.md` specialist profiles (inlined into implementer/tdd-guide prompts) were rewritten from authoritative agent-guideline sources, cloned/read in full rather than keyword-grepped. Go: JetBrains [go-modern-guidelines](https://github.com/JetBrains/go-modern-guidelines) (vendored at `docs/references/go-modern-guidelines` for future updates) — version discipline (detect the Go version from go.mod FIRST; use only features ≤ that version; modern idioms are authoritative over nearby legacy code, skippable only when they'd not compile or change behavior) plus the full 47-guideline catalog distilled into version-gated idiom sections (1.21 stdlib helpers → 1.22 `range n`/`cmp.Or`/loopvar → 1.23 iterators/`slices.Sorted` → 1.24 `omitzero`/`b.Loop` → 1.25 `wg.Go` → 1.26 `new(42)`/`errors.AsType` → 1.27 `encoding/json/v2`/`CutLast`/stdlib `uuid`/promoted fields). Python: Trail of Bits modern-python skill — uv-only workflow (`uv add`/`uv run`, never manual venv activation or hand-edited dependency lists), ruff replacing flake8+black+isort, `ty` type-checking, PEP 735 `[dependency-groups]`, PEP 723 scripts, 3.10–3.13 syntax gating, tautological-test ban. Rust: Microsoft Pragmatic Rust Guidelines (agent edition) — `From` impls over `map_err`, panics-for-detected-bugs semantics, strong types (`PathBuf` not `String`), `async fn` over `impl Future`, concrete>generic>`dyn` escalation ladder, `#[expect]` over `#[allow]`, macros-as-last-resort, edition-2024-for-new-crates, no meta design docs, tautological-test ban. Backend (TS/Node): `satisfies`/const type params, Error `cause`, `structuredClone`/`groupBy`/iterator helpers/`AbortSignal`, engines-version detection, no unrequested upgrades. Frontend: version discipline + platform-APIs-over-dependencies + React 19 `use()`/`useOptimistic`. Tests pin the distilled tokens (11 lang-profile assertions); README sources noted.

### Fixed
- **v0.3.24 (owner-aware convergence + RED-boundary reliability)** — two incident classes from runs 2026-08-28T12-51-40 (greenfield Kotlin RED loop) and 13-04-28 (9-round requirements deadlock): (S1) the artifact/spec approval gates were owner-blind — `reviewHasBlockingVerdictFinding` blocked on ANY blocking finding including downstream-owned ones the stage cannot fix; now only own/upstream-owned actionable findings block, and a fully-carried verdict exits `CONVERGED-CARRIED` with the debt delivered via `deliverCarriedDebt` (pending replan requests + `bumpOwnerRevision` for routable owners, ledger injection + disclosure for non-routable; only genuine approval green-skips — the revision-gate fast-forward can never green-skip a carried artifact). (S2) route-back re-entry resets the segment round budget (`routeBackReentry`) under the global 3× ceiling. (S3) escalate-now accepts any non-empty evidence (quote/note/text/detail). (S4) RED-boundary path matching is suffix/prefix-tolerant for agent-echoed absolute paths (exactly-one-candidate rule keeps same-basename ambiguity conservative), `redEvidenceSignature` excludes harness runtime-evidence + spec-dir paths so cycle detection fires, the late-recovery judge floor widens to `[fix-environment, allow-scaffold]`, and RED cleanup never reverts harness bookkeeping (`!isHarnessBookkeepingPath` on `restoreUnacceptedRedChanges` — implementation-evidence.jsonl survived only by luck before).
- **v0.3.23 (configurable output language)** — `"language": "english"` in `~/.super-dev/config.json` (env `SUPER_DEV_LANGUAGE` overrides) makes every agent-written artifact (documents, summaries, control text fields, commit messages) honor the configured language via a directive appended at the `realAgent` prompt seam + a dedicated `buildReflectionTask` path for the direct-session reflection call; non-string config values degrade to English, control-key extraction is unaffected.
- **v0.3.22 (phase-leak guard, route caps, salvage, advisory signal)** — cross-phase deliverable leakage is now a BLOCKING pre-commit check (full git delta minus the current phase's declared scope, revert via `restorePaths`); judge timeout 240s → 480s; `SUPER_DEV_MAX_RED_JUDGE_ROUTES` caps RED-loop judge routings (post-cap floor `fix-environment`); research `openIssues` became optional (schema + prompt control line) so the missing-key error degrades instead of killing Stage 3; `changedNotClaimed` advisory filters harness-bookkeeping noise while `claimedNotChanged` stays strict.
- **v0.3.21 (honest bring-up, sanitization, cancellation trails)** — static sites (index.html, no package.json scripts) get a fixed-port static-server ladder (python3/caddy/npx serve) instead of the doomed `npm run dev` fallbacks, and map to a disclosed `skipped-static` integration outcome that passes when review+build are green; `sanitizeDiscoveredCmd` strips `#` comments and rejects prose/overlong discovered commands before they reach the shell; readiness races child-exit so dead candidates fail fast; `task()` discards results returning after an abort and records an honest `cancelled` row; `classifyStage` tags every deterministic fallback `fallback: true` with a WARN log; terminal cancellation writes a `run.cancelled` audit row.
- **v0.3.19 (auto-route upstream-owned blockers)** — a convergence blocker whose own analysis already names the fix path (exactly ONE routable strictly-upstream owner, e.g. run 2026-08-27T00-59-52's BDD-F-001 owner=requirements with a crisp recommendation) still burned a full HITL round-trip only for the user to confirm the route-back the analysis had already chosen — since M5 every non-run-level choice routes identically, so the wait was ceremonial. When `planInlineRouteBack` resolves a jump AND `autoRouteBackEnabled()` (default ON; kill-switch `SUPER_DEV_NO_AUTO_ROUTEBACK=1` / alias `SUPER_DEV_AUTO_ROUTEBACK=0` restores the prompt byte-identically), the loop now throws the RouteBackSignal DIRECTLY and writes the same escalation report with a machine-taken `route-back-auto` decision for audit. Ambiguous shapes (multi-owner, non-routable owner, exhausted edge budget, kill-switch) keep the HITL escalation unchanged; the escalation retry budget is NOT consumed by an auto-route (the route-back carries its own per-edge journal budget + `SUPER_DEV_MAX_INLINE_JUMPS`). README: auto-route documented in the escalation-ladder section + the env table (`SUPER_DEV_NO_AUTO_ROUTEBACK` / `SUPER_DEV_AUTO_ROUTEBACK`).
- **v0.3.18 (double-index spec ids)** — the slug LLM echoing a referenced requirement FILENAME (`docs/requirements/16-dimension-financials.md` → slug "16-dimension-financials") made setup prepend its own allocated number into `16-16-dimension-financials`. Specs 01–15 escaped only by nondeterministic luck (the model happened to rephrase, e.g. `02-data-persistence.md` → `data-persistence-layer`); the prompt never barred numeral echoes. Two layers: (1) content-aware slugs — `taskFileExcerpts` feeds up to 3 referenced files' CONTENT (~6 KB each) into the slug prompt, which now names the work by subject matter rather than file names and forbids index numerals; (2) a deterministic `dedupeSlugIndex` guard on both fresh-allocation branches (normal + `SUPER_DEV_NO_SPEC_REUSE=1`) strips leading slug numerals only when they provably echo a docs-path spec reference in the task (`specRefNumerals`), so free-text identity numerals (`254-step-e2e-dashboard`) survive verbatim for track reuse. Also skips the eager LLM slug call when the task re-enters an existing spec track via `docs/specifications/NN-…` (that branch never allocates a fresh id).
- **v0.3.17 (conftest.py hollow-guard exemption)** — the hollow-assertion guard's `TEST_FILE_NAME_RE` classified ANY file under `tests/` as a test file, so pytest's canonical support artifact — `conftest.py` (auto-loaded fixtures/sys.path bootstrap, legitimately assertion-free) — was flagged as a hollow test and `restoreUnacceptedRedChanges` deleted the ENTIRE RED set. Run 2026-08-26T02-36-42-419Z phase-02 lost two REAL REDs this way (tries 3 and 5: oracle `status=red`, 17–19 intended missing-behavior failures, conftest boundary-allowed) while the agent's only clean import fix kept being destroyed. Fix: basename exemption `(^|\/)conftest\.py$` for conftests that pass the test-file gate (any tests/ depth; package-root conftest is exempt via the first gate already). Review remediation: the vacuous any-depth test was reframed into a first-gate control plus an explicit deep-nesting pin, and the sibling gap — a package-root conftest still denied by the RED boundary classifier — is recorded in the plan doc's backlog. Same run's sibling gaps documented as backlog designs in the plan doc: `fix-environment` routed from red-no-progress has no executor in the RED loop, and the oracle-vs-agent pytest invocation mismatch (bare `pytest` vs `python3 -m pytest`) is command-blindness the retry hint never surfaces.
- **v0.3.16 (RED-loop timeout honesty)** — four cooperating defects turned tdd-guide wall-clock timeouts into a doom loop (run 2026-08-23T02-59-20-670Z: 15/26 tdd calls died at exactly 1200s; phases 3/5/6 burned 4–6 tries each on misleading feedback). F1: a timed-out tdd-guide (`control=no`) now clears `testFiles` instead of echoing the previous try's claim — the log previously printed the lie `error=timed out … test files=tests/screen.test.ts`, the oracle ran vitest on a cleanup-deleted ghost file (`No test files found` → misleading `red-broken`), and the retry hint sent the agent hunting a compile defect that did not exist. F2: a RED **review** that never completed (timeout/error OR no-usable-verdict) no longer deletes the written (never-adjudicated) test file — the `review-weak` restore path is skipped for both non-completion templates (real weak/contradiction verdicts keep RC8 restore semantics). Review remediation: the F4 disk-state line now probes the disk (claim ∪ last-claimed ∪ red-changed) instead of the cleared claim, and agent-death retries drop the misleading stock "tests did not compile" template. F3: `buildTddPrompt` carries a Deadline-survival block (write the test file to disk EARLY — a written-but-unverified file survives a timeout, prose does not; ~10-tool-call exploration cap; retry-after-timeout skips re-exploration). F4: the retry hint is prefixed with the honest death cause (`PREVIOUS TRY DIED AT THE WALL CLOCK`), the current disk state, and write-first ordering. RED-first: 7/8 tests fail on the pre-fix tree; the 8th is the RC8 no-regression control. Also fills the README Configuration gap for `SUPER_DEV_BUILD_TIMEOUT_MS` / `SUPER_DEV_BUILD_TEST_PACKAGES` (pre-existing docs-test failure).
- **v0.3.15 (config.json env channel)** — all ~40 user-facing `SUPER_DEV_*` tunables (judge timeout/calls, replan rounds, red retries, bootstrap/build timeouts, model/thinking/backend selectors, every kill-switch) are now settable in `~/.super-dev/config.json` under an `env` map, consumed via the new `superDevEnv()` accessor with precedence process.env > config.env > unset. GUI-launched pi sessions (no shell env) finally have a persistent channel. Excluded: `SUPER_DEV_DIR` (bootstrap) and subprocess IPC / release-tooling plumbing vars. Empty-string env vars are treated as unset so they cannot mask a configured value.
- **v0.3.14 (20-minute agent wall clock)** — `DEFAULT_SPAWN_TIMEOUT_MS` 480s → 1200s, aligning every non-code-writing role (spec/bdd/research/review writers) with the code-writing 20-minute cap. Root cause: big-spec writers spend ~70% of the budget re-verifying upstream anchors, then run out of the 480s hard wall mid-compose — the timeout discards the entire `structured_output` (control=null) and the retry feedback mislabels it as a schema violation, looping (run 2026-08-23T00-59-32-122Z spec rounds 2/4). Code-writing agents were already 1200s; judge (240s, `SUPER_DEV_JUDGE_TIMEOUT_MS`) and replan-lead timeouts are separately tuned and unchanged.
- **v0.3.13** — the prototype stage was mislabeled "Stage 6B" in run logs (a collision with the design-review stage that owns 6B); relabeled "Stage 6C — Prototype" per the design → review → prototype → spec order.
- **v0.3.12 (spec-reuse absorption)** — the anchor-Jaccard reuse branch gained the numeric-verbatim guard it never had (the 06 task was absorbed into the merged 05 track at Jaccard 0.643 because only the slug branch carried the R6 fix; anchor spec-reference numerals must now appear in the candidate task), and a git-confirmed merge writes `.complete` + clears the resume cache at the moment git confirms — merged tracks can never stay live reuse/resume candidates when the run later cancels or ends partial at close-out.
- **v0.3.11 (codebase sweep-3)** — 12-agent whole-codebase review (110 findings → 45 groups): Go RED oracle file-target blocker (package dirs, in-package tests can now confirm RED/GREEN), 64MB maxBuffer on gate spawns, defaultBranch plumbing to all scoping consumers, language-aware `#` comment stripping, anchored alias relaxation, judge budgets per-run, `addressed` stays blocking until verified, Stage-10 replay guard extended to the outer convergence boundary (deferred route-back/blocked-on-decisions), deriveRunStatus extracted (ever-failed→last-status, absent buildGate no longer vacuous success, success clears stale __stagnated), retry-with-guidance never resets the live checkout, unconditional harness-bookkeeping git excludes, corrective respawn re-derives @file delivery + fences prior output, run events carry the RUN uuid (INV-L4/L5), stage lifecycle opens on skip/budget (INV-L6), spec-token RED boundary narrowed, updateStats single-call + run-pinned audit path, atomic knowledge/user-notes writes, per-kind escalation reports + budget-exhausted report, spec-convergence cap-path judge, canonical owner fingerprints in the duty shield + replan dedupe, tolerant render-schema boolean ingest.
- **Stage 10/11 resume fidelity (V1)** — replayed rounds no longer arm terminal
  exits. Resuming a run that died at verification with two identical reviewer
  verdicts used to reconstruct those failures from the resume cache and
  immediately stagnation-break BEFORE any fresh agent call (run
  2026-08-21T07-20-57-254Z, escalation 3 — the only remedy was hand-deleting
  the `pipeline.verify.*` cache rows). The wired Stage 10 choke point
  (`recordVerificationStagnation` in `verificationConvergenceNode`) plus the
  legacy `reviewLoopUntil` and Stage 11's `recordTestStagnation` now classify
  each observation/attempt as replay-derived when
  the persisted occurrence count covers it (gated on a genuine resume — a
  reused track with stale rows is never excluded — and the
  `SUPER_DEV_NO_VERIFY_REPLAY_GUARD=1` kill-switch): replayed observations
  reconstruct state but only FRESH observations arm stagnation and the
  dead-state break, mirroring the writer loops' F3 contract
  ("replayed rounds do not consume the fresh budget"). Fresh-run behavior is
  byte-identical (parity pinned). Review round 1 (both reviewers Changes
  Requested) caught the fix initially targeting compatibility-only nodes with
  an unpopulated gate — remediated onto the wired node with the ctx.options
  gate source. Round 2 verified both blockers closed and flagged a stale
  arms memo across route-back re-entry — fixed via the cumulative attempts
  ledger (re-entry attempts stay fresh). 13 new tests; RED-first with the
  final file: 9 fix-specific failures pre-fix, 4 controls pass.

- M5 emulation retirement (v0.3.9) — migration complete: the interactive decision suppression is DELETED (upstream-owned blockers route regardless of retry-with-guidance/revise-manually choices; abandon and accept-limitation remain the only genuine human overrides; guidance from a routed retry-with-guidance persists to user-notes for the owner), and the replan emulation is retired for routing at every owner-addressable site (artifact/spec/verify convergence + the walker's create-requests tier): a declined jump is an honest fatal naming the exhausted edge with NO automatic process restart, no __replan marker, and no replan-requests.json write. The emulation survives only for (a) the walker's pending-rows cross-run resume tier, (b) the implementation-RED replan-lead site (findings carry no structured ownerStage — the LLM lead resolves it), and (c) genuinely interrupted runs. Kill-switch SUPER_DEV_NO_INLINE_ROUTEBACK=1 now means “no automatic route-back” (escalation surface / honest fatal) instead of “byte-identical emulation.” 10 new/updated pins; RED-first: 9 fix-specific failures pre-fix, 109 controls pass (118 tests).
- M4 all-producers + escalation route-back choice (v0.3.8): planInlineRouteBack's pilot `from` allowlist is RETIRED — every routable producer (requirements/bdd/research/design convergence, spec, verify, implementation) can throw when findings hold exactly one strictly-upstream routable owner with edge budget. The escalation surface (G6) gains `route-back` as an EscalationChoice: a route-back-eligible failure offers “Route back to ⟨owner⟩ (recommended)” FIRST (soft + hard), persisted to escalation-report.md with the offered list (MP5); a user-chosen route-back throws RouteBackSignal inline, degrades to the replan emulation on an exhausted edge, and fatals honestly if both decline (applyRetryDecision untouched — it only acts on retry-with-guidance). Verify's blocked-on-decisions seam is inline-first (deferred findings carry ownerStage → jump before maybeTriggerReplan). The implementation env-blocker override arm now consults classifyJudgeRoute (single vocabulary, zero behavior change). 13 new pins; RED-first: 13 fail pre-fix, 119 controls pass.
- M3 inline route-back default-ON + G4 revision-gate + resume fidelity (v0.3.7): the pilot incident edges (bdd→upstream, spec→upstream) now route back IN-PROCESS by default — kill-switch `SUPER_DEV_NO_INLINE_ROUTEBACK=1` restores the replan emulation byte-identical (G8 transfers to kill-switch equivalence). Spec-convergence gained the pilot throw-site (upstream-signature-repeat branch). New `src/routing/revision-gate.ts`: convergence stages whose artifact revision is unchanged after a jump FAST-FORWARD with zero agent calls (cheap deterministic re-validation only; validator-less research conservatively re-runs; inert on never-jumped tracks). Resume fidelity (MP1): a resumed track seeds its routing-budget epoch from the journal so a crashed run's jumps are never re-armed. 19 new pins; RED-first verified (17 fail pre-fix, controls pass).
- M2 addressable walker + journal (v0.3.6): `withInlineRouteBack(PIPELINE_CHILDREN)` replaces the bare sequence as the pipeline root — flag OFF (default) pure pass-through (G8 byte-identical), flag ON (`SUPER_DEV_INLINE_ROUTEBACK=1`) catches `RouteBackSignal` above the walk, journals the jump synchronously before re-entry (MP1), drops owner+downstream resume-cache rows (G5), bumps the owner revision, injects blocker findings as round-1 replan requests, and sub-walks `children[indexOf(owner)..]` (G1) — pre-owner stages never re-run. New `src/routing/journal.ts` (persisted budget authority MP2, torn-line tolerance, fail-closed charge). Pilot throw-site wired in artifact-convergence's upstream-owned headless branch (single strictly-upstream routable owner + per-edge budget); convergence nodes now carry addressable `id`s. Bounded by per-edge cap 2 and `SUPER_DEV_MAX_INLINE_JUMPS` (default 4).
- M1 routing vocabulary (v0.3.5): new `src/routing/router.ts` — normalized `RoutingCommand` (continue/retry/route-back/escalate/accept-limitation/abort), budget-gated `routeBackOrEscalate` (per-edge cap 2, persisted-journal-backed per MP2), `RouteBackSignal extends FatalAbort` (G2 — airtight propagation, zero combinator changes), journal types with walk-position fields (G3), and pure classification tables mapping escalation choices, all 8 judge routes, and findings onto the one vocabulary. Zero production call sites (M1 of the routing-architecture migration; incident 2026-08-21T03-23-47-913Z).

### Changed

- **v0.3.4 — Shape-dual real-LLM convergence benchmark (cumora lesson: statistical shape-duals so a principle regression breaks exactly one shape; harness impersonates a misbehaving writer, never calls LLMs on its own; honest cost tables).** New `src/bench/convergence-bench.ts`: both shapes seed the SAME minimal track (temp spec dir, 3-AC requirements + 3-scenario BDD) and drive the REAL spec-convergence loop with a REAL ctx (agent() → the production session backend; helper() → the deterministic gates); the ONLY difference is the writer's scenario prelude — **converges-when-should** seeds a semantic contradiction (spec note "the URL field remains visible" vs AC-02 hiding) that the writer must resolve after the honest reviewer blocks (catches never-passing gates, never-approving reviewers, verdict laundering), while **holds-firm-when-should** pins the same contradiction but the writer fake-fixes forever (rephrase, claim, keep the sentence — catching duty-downgrade over-firing on genuine semantic blockers, laundered rejections, and gate bypasses). Detection is deterministic (`/URL field remains visible/i` on the final rendered spec); criteria are statistical (converges pass-rate ≥ 0.8 with zero harness errors; holds-firm requires ZERO approvals while the defect survives — cap outcomes are its expected terminal); every trial reports agent calls + wall time and the report prints the honest total-cost line and a smoke-vs-statistical validity warning. Gated per the P-06 commitment: the real-LLM bench runs ONLY under `SUPER_DEV_BENCH=1` (`vitest run` never spawns LLMs), with `SUPER_DEV_BENCH_TRIALS` (default 1 = smoke; ≥3 for statistical claims) and `SUPER_DEV_BENCH_TIMEOUT_MS`. The deterministic layer (criteria, preludes, detection, report, gating — 8 tests) ships in the normal suite. Run: `SUPER_DEV_BENCH=1 npx vitest run tests/convergence-bench-v0.3.4.test.ts -t "real bench"`. Plan doc docs/requirements/043-shape-dual-benchmark-v0.3.4.md.

- **v0.3.3 — Ledger persistence + completion audit (cumora agent-team lesson: state that outlives the loop; Codex close-out: treat completion as a claim to verify).** The convergence ledger was a per-run in-memory object — a resume/restart replayed cached rounds but the ledger restarted empty, losing every unresolved finding the prior run recorded. **(L1)** every ledger mutation now best-effort persists to `specDir/.convergence-ledger.json` (versioned, keyed by the `.task` anchor hash so a different task on the same track inherits nothing; registered harness bookkeeping + git-excluded like `.run-lock`), and round 1 of every convergence loop injects the prior run's unresolved residue — open/needs-human BLOCKING findings plus addressed-but-unverified claims (nobody will verify them after a restart) — as `[prior-run finding …]` feedback lines through the same fingerprint-deduped seam as pending replan requests (idempotent across repeated restarts, cap 8 with the remainder announced). **(V2)** new `src/completion-audit.ts` writes a deterministic `completion-audit.md` for EVERY run outcome at summary derivation — status, phases, review/build/integration/merge outcomes, deferred findings, accepted limitations, ledger residue — and records an AUDIT ANOMALY section when a run claims success with unresolved BLOCKING findings (a gate hole made visible). Records only; the merge gates remain the enforcers. RED-first: the 7 L1 tests fail on pre-fix code (V2 module tests are green-on-new-module by construction — the new file survives the src stash — plus a workflow wiring revert-canary); suite 2614 → 2629, tsc strict-clean. Plan doc docs/requirements/042-ledger-persistence-completion-audit-v0.3.3.md.

- **v0.3.2 — Contract-Claims Layer (WS-1 rung-2 sensors; run `2026-08-20T06-19-50-494Z`: the filename-allowlist defect class was machine-checkable from round 1 — enumerate registry values × derive filenames × match against the declared pattern — yet no checker ran it and three review rounds discovered it one site at a time).** Grounded in the research synthesis (RSI survey §5.2: wherever a checker exists, refinement is rebuilt around it; Self-Correction Illusion: addressability; pre-execution structural checks beat unstructured critique) and the reference-repos full read (deepseek-harness verify-type-equiv: claims artifacts make about code are checked mechanically, never by reviewer goodwill). **(C1)** the design control gains a `contracts` block ({name, pattern, enumerates[], sourceAnchor?, derivationRule?, uniqueness?} — the declared paired generate/validate contract WITH its enumerated closure) plus `alternativesConsidered` (dsh: a decision recorded without what it beat invites re-litigation); a new `designComplete` validator — the design stage's FIRST deterministic gate — checks every pattern compiles, EVERY enumerated value matches its own pattern (all violations in one table: the run-06-19 kill at round 1), the sourceAnchor path exists with optional #export verification (cheap verify-type-equiv), and uniqueness claims hold; it also enforces render parity (control declares contracts ⇒ rendered doc carries the `## Contract Claims` section — the control-had-data-the-template-dropped class). **(C2)** `deliverablesPreflightErrors` in gate-spec-trace: requireContains/requireNotContains patterns must compile, requireScenarios ids must exist in the BDD doc, paths repo-relative, test names non-empty — malformed deliverables fail at SPEC time instead of burning a phase-GREEN attempt. **(C3)** `bddBoundaryLintErrors` in gate-bdd: every numeric bound an AC pins (at most/at least/exactly/top/first/within/max/min/capped-at N) must be named by some scenario (digit-normalized, ≤4 reported) — the rethink's BDD-F01 class, mechanically. **(C4)** ACs gain optional `verifiedBy: deterministic|test|manual` (schema + prompt + render; consumption deferred). Reviewer division-of-labor line (dsh): when a Contract Claims section exists, the checker owns closure/anchors — the reviewer reads the cited source and verifies the ENUMERATION MATCHES REALITY. v0.3.0 philosophy intact: deterministic sensors → retry feedback through existing gates; no judge, no new loops. RED-first: all 19 new tests fail on pre-fix code; suite 2586 → 2605 (3 pre-existing environment-dependent failures reproduced on clean HEAD). Plan doc docs/requirements/041-contract-claims-layer-v0.3.2.md. `npx tsc --noEmit` strict-clean.

- **v0.3.1 — class-aware feedback + reviewer calibration (run `2026-08-20T06-19-50-494Z`: one systemic artifact-name-validation defect class surfaced one filename family per review round across 4 rounds — site-addressed feedback produces whack-a-mole; refinement projects toward the refiner's framing).** Grounded in the full-read reference study (docs/requirements/039-reference-repos-full-read-v0.3.x.md: Codex's production review rubric, deepseek-harness's gate/reviewer division of labor, cumora's truncation accounting + exit-not-close settle, SWE-agent ACI, the Self-Correction Illusion's addressability result). **(F1)** review findings gain an optional `defectClass` (schema + convergence ledger, merge-preserved) and the reviewer duty to name generalizing defects as a class; both `compactReviewFindings` now pass reviewer EVIDENCE through (first 2 items, 240-char cap with elision markers) so the writer re-verifies the way the reviewer falsified it, tag `class=<name>`, and ANNOUNCE every truncation past the 8-finding cap instead of silently dropping;
